# Agents · I · part 7

1,033 entries, implementer to implementer.agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **implementer**: Claude Sonnet worker that makes one bounded change from a written contract: objective, files, required change, constraints, non-goals, interfaces to keep, acceptance criteria. Use when the approach is already decided. Pass `isolated: true` when another writer runs at the same time. Not for design choices, open-ended debugging or ambiguous requirements; the lead decides those. ([mcgeerdev/.dotfiles](https://github.com/mcgeerdev/.dotfiles/blob/HEAD/wow/.omp/agent/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Executes a defined implementation task — code edits, refactors, config changes (anything that ships, deploys, or runs in CI). Use PROACTIVELY for behavioral changes to production code paths, infra definitions, or CI config. Give it a concrete spec (goal, files, approach, acceptance criteria), not an open question. ([mcurcio/skills](https://github.com/mcurcio/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/workflow/agents/implementer.md))

- **implementer**: The most active archetype. Reads the task contract, the planner's plan (if any), the reviewer repair recommendations (if a repair attempt), and the current state. Returns one or more `effect_proposals` — structured data describing what the coordinator should change. The implementer never edits files, never runs mutating commands, never applies effects. ([mhingston/orchestrator](https://github.com/mhingston/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Use for moderately hard implementation — non-trivial logic, multi-step refactors, stateful components, cross-file changes that need real thought but NOT top-tier horsepower. The middle rung between the main session and complex-implementer. Runs at sonnet/high WITHOUT changing your session model/effort. Do NOT use for trivial copy (text-and-copy-editor), pure CSS/styling polish (visual-polish), or genuinely hard work — complex animation/canvas/WebGL/particles, tricky algorithms, perf-critical rendering, intricate concurrency (complex-implementer, opus/high). ([michael-v17/model-routing-kit](https://github.com/michael-v17/model-routing-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Use this agent BEFORE implementing any new feature, contract, repository, specification, UoW variant, or component in MicroKit.Persistence. Produces a detailed implementation plan — file structure, interface design, public API surface, EF Core mapping strategy, dependency impact, test strategy — and waits for explicit approval before any code is written. Invoke it when asked to implement, create, add, or build something new. Do NOT use for architecture arbitration, dependency checks, or performance analysis — use the dedicated agents for those. ([michaelatsey/microkit](https://github.com/michaelatsey/microkit/blob/HEAD/modules/MicroKit.Persistence/.claude/agents/implementer.md))
- **implementer**: Implements one well-specified GitHub issue or spec (refactor, split, mechanical change, scoped feature) and runs the checks of the areas it touches. - **implementer-complex**: The explicit high-complexity escalation lane for judgment-heavy, high-risk, context-heavy, or wide-blast-radius work. Spawned only by a declared delegate or full route. ([toshon-jennings/agent-advisor](https://github.com/toshon-jennings/agent-advisor/blob/HEAD/platforms/claude/plugins/claude-advisor/agents/implementer-complex.md))
- **implementer-contract**: Domain implementer for grid-contract — proto schemas, codegen, fixture generation, gridwire reference library (header, fragmentation, GTC1 compression, crypto). Use after test-author has written the tests, to turn them green. ([yyamandevrim/grid-contract](https://github.com/yyamandevrim/grid-contract/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-contract.md))
- **implementer-cpp**: Implements one scoped section of a project's implementation plan (code, tests, plan update) on the current branch, as briefed by the /implement-cpp skill. Does not commit or push. ([armandli/get-skilled](https://github.com/armandli/get-skilled/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer-cpp.md))
- **implementer-critical**: 高リスク境界の実装を担当する Claude teammate。DB migration・並行処理・認可・セキュリティ・境界横断変更を実装・検証する。コミットはしない ([mizulba-dev/dev-method](https://github.com/mizulba-dev/dev-method/blob/HEAD/src/plugin-claude/agents/implementer-critical.md))
- **implementer-critical**: Implements one high-risk step of an Agentium plan (sandbox and denied paths, money and consent, persistence and recovery, concurrency) in its own worktree and hands back commits, checks and limitations; the coordinator reviews, opens the PR and integrates. ([pigeaca/Agentium](https://github.com/pigeaca/Agentium/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-critical.md))
- **implementer-critical**: 壊すと再起動試験・追試で失格になり得る改善1項目（メモリに状態を持つインメモリ化・非同期化・まとめての書き込み・アルゴリズムの変更・複数台化に伴うコード変更・初期化 API に触る変更）を git worktree で設計・実装し、build・vet・test を通して1コミットにし、ブランチを push して Pull Request を作って返す。デプロイはしない。メインのセッションが目的と根拠と整合性の前提を決めた後に、Agent ツールの isolation "worktree" 付きで使う。 ([tatsuru/isucon2026-public](https://github.com/tatsuru/isucon2026-public/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-critical.md))
- **implementer-css-architecture**: pragmatist css-architecture implementer — composed from the pragmatist personality x css-architecture domain x implementer phase via /guild-compile. ([krambuhl/agents](https://github.com/krambuhl/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/guild/agents/implementer-css-architecture.md))
- **implementer-data**: Phase 2 データ取得層実装担当。lib/内のヘルパーとServer Actions・Route Handlersをタスク内容に沿って実装する。他レイヤー(db/ui)のファイルは変更しない。 ([huuriekaiseki-sketch/riff-gear](https://github.com/huuriekaiseki-sketch/riff-gear/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-data.md))
- **implementer-database**: Implementatore di un task dell'area database (migrazioni, schema, policy RLS, funzioni del database ed edge function di Supabase), lanciato da tollgate:execute con il percorso del brief. ([ginz0ls/tollgate](https://github.com/ginz0ls/tollgate/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer-database.md))
- **implementer-db**: Phase 2 DB層実装担当。supabase/migrations/にタスク内容に沿ったマイグレーション(テーブル・RLS・GRANT・RPC)を追加する。他レイヤー(data/ui)のファイルは変更しない。 ([huuriekaiseki-sketch/riff-gear](https://github.com/huuriekaiseki-sketch/riff-gear/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-db.md))
- **implementer-deep**: Implement tickets that touch funds, authority, on-chain code, migrations, or concurrency, or that leave a design decision open. ([bryzettler/dotfiles](https://github.com/bryzettler/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/config/claude/agents/implementer-deep.md))
- **implementer-deep**: Complex-task and difficult-bug implementation seat (heavy-reasoning rung). ([DennisLoska/skills](https://github.com/DennisLoska/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer-deep.md))
- **implementer-deep**: Nur nach zwei gescheiterten implementer-Versuchen oder bei unbekannter Fehlerursache über mehrere Subsysteme. ([Hasim-Uner/meine-wordpress-site-2fe6f514](https://github.com/Hasim-Uner/meine-wordpress-site-2fe6f514/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-deep.md))
- **implementer-deep**: Executes one high-complexity impl-flow implementation plan (real design judgement required while implementing) on opus at xhigh effort. - **implementer-deep**: Stage 6 of pitwall. Implements one high risk change - auth, payments, data migrations or deletion, infrastructure, security - or the fix of a bug whose cause is unknown, following the preloaded epic skill with the extra care it asks for there, and returns a DONE, PARTIAL or NEEDS_OWNER report. Dispatched by /pitwall:continue, one per change at a time, within a call budget. ([Perchusha/pitwall](https://github.com/Perchusha/pitwall/blob/HEAD/plugins/pitwall/agents/implementer-deep.md))
- **implementer-deep**: Implements a single tracked issue inside an isolated git worktree — edits files and verifies the work through the orchestrate capability tools. Deep-effort variant for complex, high-risk issues. Spawned by the orchestrate skill; not invoked directly. ([rodrigorjsf/agent-engineering-toolkit](https://github.com/rodrigorjsf/agent-engineering-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/orchestrate/agents/implementer-deep.md))
- **implementer-deep**: Runs the implement step of a software-dev task. Writes the code for the approved plan in small steps and commits each green step through thanhtan. Takes the plan items whose tier is deep (and those with no tier). Same contract as `implementer-sol.md`, run natively rather than dispatched — no Codex hop, since Opus is the model doing the work directly: builds ONE chunk from its eight-field brief inside the isolated capsule the step names, TDD (red first, green after), and authors the chunk's JIT journey script when the designer nominated one, with the anti-brittleness checklist enforced on its own output. - **implementer-opus**: Build an approved cover sheet by executing its control flow step by step, run QA through the verifier, and open the PR. The only writer on the branch. ([greenfield-inc/agent-farm](https://github.com/greenfield-inc/agent-farm/blob/HEAD/plugins/bigbrain/agents/implementer-opus.md) · ★9)
- **implementer-opus**: Builds one ticket again on Opus after the implementer failed on it, in its own worktree, from the first attempt's report and worktree. Never commits; the session commits. Reports its status, its worktree, a suggested commit subject and the evidence. The filled prompt is the complete task. ([likeahuman-ai/development-advanced](https://github.com/likeahuman-ai/development-advanced/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer-opus.md) · ★1)
- **implementer-opus**: Scoped implementation agent, Opus tier, the default for dispatched work. Use to implement a single Section of Work from an approved spec whose body names no reason for another tier - well-bounded work with a clear contract, multi-file coordination, a refactor seam, a performance-sensitive path, or ambiguity within a clear design. Dispatch with a brief built from the executing-work skill's Dispatch Brief template. Escalates ambiguity rather than guessing. ([SApplefeld/Plugins](https://github.com/SApplefeld/Plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/grimoire/agents/implementer-opus.md) · ★1)
- **implementer-opus**: Implements one pipeline ticket end to end and opens its own PR. Spawned by the pipeline loop for tickets routed to opus. ([TheLazyLemur/pipeline-plugin](https://github.com/TheLazyLemur/pipeline-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer-opus.md) · ★1)
- **implementer-opus**: Implementer tier L (Opus, high effort). Implements exactly one plan from docs/plans/ whose "Implementer tier" is L (saves, money, security, concurrency). Codes and tests only; makes no design decisions. ([Valentindghjg/roblox-game-template](https://github.com/Valentindghjg/roblox-game-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-opus.md) · ★1)
- **implementer-opus**: General implementation worker on Opus 5.5, in its own git worktree or the non-git paths a brief names. Medium effort by default, the first Claude rung for every coding leg; the lead sets the Agent call's `effort` to xhigh for the retry rung and for React / component work. Implements, verifies with the tests named in the brief, commits on its own branch when in git. Never pushes, merges, or deploys. ([BigCactusLabs/agent-skills](https://github.com/BigCactusLabs/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/orchestrator/agents/implementer-opus.md))
- **implementer-opus**: implementer at opus, effort high — 3+ file integration, novel patterns, high-stakes work (auth, payments, migrations, deletes). Dispatch named. ([Crawford-Young/claude-config](https://github.com/Crawford-Young/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer-opus.md))
- **implementer-opus**: [opus] Implements tasks from a plan inside its feature folder (.worktrees/&lt;area&gt;-&lt;slug&gt;, branch feat/&lt;area&gt;-&lt;slug&gt;), one commit per task, following the verification list in AGENTS.md. Doesn't push, merge or apply shared changes. Use it in step 3 of the SDD flow or to fix what the reviewer returns. ([danielsaed/sdd-base](https://github.com/danielsaed/sdd-base/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/implementer-opus.md))
- **implementer-opus**: Scoped implementation agent, Opus tier. Dispatched by executing-work to implement one delegate-capable Section of Work - moderate complexity such as multi-file coordination, nuanced refactoring, performance-sensitive logic, or mild ambiguity within a clear design. Brief it with the spec path and section name, the files in scope, the acceptance criteria, the style-skill file paths, the test expectation, and the build/test commands. - **implementer-opus**: Opus implementer for the Rust that matters - rh-sim tick order, state, RNG, save/restore/hash; wire-format changes; rh-persist/rh-placement migrations; security hardening of rh-server (admission, auth, account API, control surface, input validation, rate limits); any cross-crate packet; and L2 escalation after Sonnet fails. - **implementer-opus**: Implements one bounded change that needs judgment - tricky logic, concurrency, security-sensitive code (auth, permissions, input validation, crypto, secrets), data migrations, or slices with design decisions left inside them. - **implementer-opus**: Same contract as implementer, on opus — for code lanes needing real judgment (tricky refactors, concurrency, gnarly bugs, design within decided direction). - **implementer-opus-high**: The executing default — locked-plan disciplined TDD of a small-to-medium change where the design ambiguity is already removed. Opus reasoning at high (not xhigh) effort. - **implementer-sonnet**: Implements one pipeline ticket end to end and opens its own PR. Spawned by the pipeline loop for tickets routed to sonnet. ([TheLazyLemur/pipeline-plugin](https://github.com/TheLazyLemur/pipeline-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer-sonnet.md) · ★1)

- **implementer-sonnet**: プランナーが策定した実装プランに従ってコードを書くエージェント（sonnet 版）。型システム・generic 推論・複数ファイル横断・Hono/Drizzle/Zod の型統合など、haiku 版が苦手とする複雑な実装で使う。設計判断はせず、計画どおりに実装する。シンプルな機械的編集なら `implementer`（haiku）の方が速くて安い。 ([nanokajs/nanoka](https://github.com/nanokajs/nanoka/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-sonnet.md))
- **implementer-sonnet**: Default implementer (Sonnet) for any single-area packet with a failing oracle on packet/&lt;ID&gt;, including C#/Unity in client-unity, CI in .github, hosting/ recipes and build scripts; also L1 escalation after the Haiku implementer fails. Validates locally; never deploys or pushes. ([Nevanyte/RogueHalla](https://github.com/Nevanyte/RogueHalla/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-sonnet.md))
- **implementer-sonnet**: Implements one clearly specified, low-risk change - boilerplate, wiring, tests, straightforward fixes, mechanical refactors of named files. Use when the plan fully specifies the work and no judgment calls remain. For tricky logic, security-sensitive code, or anything ambiguous, use implementer-opus instead. ([scbgitosu/dev-kit](https://github.com/scbgitosu/dev-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer-sonnet.md))
- **implementer-sonnet**: Bounded implementer for frugal orchestration. Use to execute a well-specified coding task (patch, function, test, mechanical change) AFTER research is done. The prompt must give it exact files/scope, an acceptance criterion, and what NOT to touch. It runs focused checks and reports the real diff. Not for open-ended design or unscoped work — those stay with the orchestrator. ([uladzemer/frugal-orchestrator](https://github.com/uladzemer/frugal-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer-sonnet.md))
- **implementer-sonnet**: Standard implementation tasks — API endpoints, pipeline changes, React components, test suites, skill/doc authoring from an approved design. The default implementer. ([VitalyVorobyev/field-explorer](https://github.com/VitalyVorobyev/field-explorer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-sonnet.md))
- **implementer-sonnet-high**: Mechanical-but-non-trivial implementer — settled design, but the change still needs care (multi-file edits, careful test work). Sonnet at high effort. Select by TASK, not by phase. ([rwsmythe/swing-trading](https://github.com/rwsmythe/swing-trading/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-sonnet-high.md) · ★4)
- **implementer-sonnet-high**: Clearwater implementer cell (Sonnet / high effort) for well-specified builds -- packages whose brief fixes the interfaces, the algorithms and the tests (I/O adapters, naming and safe writing, presets and settings, GUI panels against stubs, fixtures, the inventory and environment tools). States scope literally; say what is out of scope in the brief. ([rwsmythe/clearwater](https://github.com/rwsmythe/clearwater/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-sonnet-high.md))
- **implementer-sonnet-med**: Mechanical-to-moderately-involved implementer — repetitive edits, boilerplate, bulk renames, and multi-file changes whose pattern is settled. Sonnet 5 at medium effort, which maps to roughly Sonnet 4.6 at high, so its task ceiling sits a notch above the old sonnet-med. Select by TASK, not by phase. ([rwsmythe/swing-trading](https://github.com/rwsmythe/swing-trading/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-sonnet-med.md) · ★4)
- **implementer-sonnet-medium**: Clearwater implementer cell (Sonnet / medium effort) for mechanical work -- running committed instruments (timings, inventories, look-check packs, review packs), fixture generation from a fixed recipe, doc formatting, and other passes whose brief leaves nothing to decide. Never for design or for a package that touches a frozen interface. ([rwsmythe/clearwater](https://github.com/rwsmythe/clearwater/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-sonnet-medium.md))
- **implementer-sonnet-xhigh**: Implementer on Sonnet at xhigh effort. The main session dispatches it to implement a planned task and to fix review findings; the dispatch prompt carries the whole assignment. Planning goes to planner-opus-xhigh; review goes to reviewer-opus-xhigh. ([powerumc/ontology](https://github.com/powerumc/ontology/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-sonnet-xhigh.md) · ★3)
- **implementer-sonnet-xhigh**: Bounded implementation agent on Claude Sonnet 5.5 at xhigh effort. Worker for hard slices: ownership, concurrency, lifecycle or failure recovery (owner decision 2026-09-28). ([MVPavan/via](https://github.com/MVPavan/via/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-sonnet-xhigh.md) · ★2)
- **implementer-sparc-coder**: implementer-sparc-coder agent for SPEK pipeline ([DNYoussef/Spek-template](https://github.com/DNYoussef/Spek-template/blob/HEAD/docs/api-reference/agents/implementer-sparc-coder.md) · ★2)
- **implementer-specification-delta**: Phase 3/4 subagent of the specification-delta pipeline. Owns the apply ↔ verify loop (stages 7–8) until verify PASS. Loads apply-specification-delta and verify-specification-delta stage skills via the Skill tool, routes back to apply on any CRITICAL finding, and only returns to the orchestrator when verify passes the hard gate (no CRITICAL, no failing test suite). Spawned only by orchestrate-specification-delta, never directly by the user. Use when the orchestrator routes to phase 3/4 of a spec delta, or when the user mentions "fase de implementación", "apply ↔ verify loop", "verify gate". ([CristianRojas-SoftwareEngineer/EvolutiveX-Agent-Orchestrator](https://github.com/CristianRojas-SoftwareEngineer/EvolutiveX-Agent-Orchestrator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-specification-delta.md))
- **implementer-standard**: Builds one task of risk class padrão (ordinary work) test-first on its own branch. Dispatched by the BACO-SDD conductor in phase 6. ([brunobaco/baco-sdd](https://github.com/brunobaco/baco-sdd/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer-standard.md) · ★3)
- **implementer-standard**: Executes one medium-complexity impl-flow implementation plan (new code in an existing pattern) on sonnet at high effort. Dispatched by /impl-flow:implement based on the plan's declared complexity, and used as the fallback when a plan declares no complexity — not for general-purpose work. ([Ogawa929/ogawa929-claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/Ogawa929/ogawa929-claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/impl-flow/agents/implementer-standard.md))
- **implementer-standard**: Implements a single tracked issue inside an isolated git worktree — edits files and verifies the work through the orchestrate capability tools. Standard-effort variant for trivial- and standard-tier issues. Spawned by the orchestrate skill; not invoked directly. ([rodrigorjsf/agent-engineering-toolkit](https://github.com/rodrigorjsf/agent-engineering-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/orchestrate/agents/implementer-standard.md))
- **implementer-step**: Execute a single implementation step from a plan. Receives pre-loaded file contents and writes code. ([samjonester/dotfiles](https://github.com/samjonester/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/personal/pi/agents/implementer-step.md) · ★5)
- **implementer-strong**: Ticket workflow role, started only by the orchestrator. Stronger model for the same task as implementer; used when implementer fails twice on the same finding or is blocked for technical reasons. Never commits or pushes. ([svenkroll/mcp-gemini-search](https://github.com/svenkroll/mcp-gemini-search/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-strong.md) · ★1)
- **implementer-strong**: 舱内实现席的升级席：职责与协议同 implementer 完全同构，缺省模型为 opus。何时用本席而非 implementer，判据单源在根 CLAUDE.md 模型分配节（升席判据①②），派单时引用条目号留痕于工单 §4。派单时须给工单路径、任务号、涉及 rules 与上游文档精确 §。规格不完备或越白名单时拒单上报，不自由发挥。 ([DingJWuyu/Picka](https://github.com/DingJWuyu/Picka/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-strong.md))
- **implementer-strong**: The strong tier of the implementer. Carries out one scoped sub-task of an approved wave plan (owned files, contract, acceptance check) and reports in a fixed short format. Briefed by a wave agent for a plan row whose Runs as column says strong. Typical work is root-cause debugging, risky refactors (concurrency, auth, migrations, published SDK API), an independent review of a wave's diff, and the one retry after a lower tier blocked. Does not plan, redesign, or touch files outside its brief. ([Peter-Ewald/agents](https://github.com/Peter-Ewald/agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-strong.md))
- **implementer-strong**: Implements exactly one kinetic ticket inside its own git worktree, test-first, runs the repo's verify command, and writes a pointer-only report file. Strong-tier variant dispatched for fix round 3. Dispatched only by /kinetic:run. ([rcmendes/kinetic](https://github.com/rcmendes/kinetic/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer-strong.md))
- **implementer-tdd-agent**: TDD implementer — generates code and tests using real TDD (RED→GREEN→REFACTOR). Chosen by the orchestrator when the project has a test suite and the design can be stated as tests first (architect's test_first: yes). implementer-coder-agent is the code-first counterpart. ([diego81b/kairos-agents-framework](https://github.com/diego81b/kairos-agents-framework/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/implementer-tdd-agent.md) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **implementer-technical**: Implements an approved plan for refactors, infrastructure, build tooling, dependencies and CI without changing user-visible behaviour unless the plan says so. ([mikaelbr/kanbot-demo](https://github.com/mikaelbr/kanbot-demo/blob/HEAD/.kanbot/agents/implementer-technical.md) · 2 copies)
- **implementer-template**: SEAM 2 -- the ONE generic implementer cell template. CHARC authors domain-tuned cells FROM this at bootstrap (e.g. implementer-&lt;model&gt;-&lt;effort&gt;), selecting by TASK reasoning-density + leverage, not by phase. Tune model/effort per cell. ([rwsmythe/clearwater](https://github.com/rwsmythe/clearwater/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-template.md))
- **implementer-test-implementer**: Writes the failing test for one acceptance criterion. Writes under tests/ and nothing else. ([RichardHightower/reliable-agentic-lab](https://github.com/RichardHightower/reliable-agentic-lab/blob/HEAD/solutions/sol2_implementer_agent_sdk/plugin/agents/implementer-test-implementer.md) · ★3)
- **implementer-test-integration**: pragmatist test-integration implementer — composed from the pragmatist personality x test-integration domain x implementer phase via /guild-compile. ([krambuhl/agents](https://github.com/krambuhl/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/guild/agents/implementer-test-integration.md))
- **implementer-test-unit**: pragmatist test-unit implementer — composed from the pragmatist personality x test-unit domain x implementer phase via /guild-compile. ([krambuhl/agents](https://github.com/krambuhl/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/guild/agents/implementer-test-unit.md))
- **implementer-tester**: Use only when explicitly requested for scoped implementer-tester work. Avoid broad scans and nested agents. ([GhAli21/cleanmatex](https://github.com/GhAli21/cleanmatex/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-tester.md))
- **implementer-tester**: Use this agent when you need to implement code changes along with comprehensive tests, execute both unit and UI tests, and provide a summary of the implementation. This agent should be called after design/planning is complete and you're ready to write production code with full test coverage.\n\nExamples:\n- &lt;example&gt;\n Context: The user has completed planning for a new feature and is ready to implement it.\n user: "I've finished designing the order status tracking feature. Can you implement it with tests?"\n assistant: "I'll use the implementer-tester agent to implement the order status track… ([GhAli21/cleanmatex](https://github.com/GhAli21/cleanmatex/blob/HEAD/.codex/agents/implementer-tester.md))
- **implementer-tests**: Use PROACTIVELY após criar ou alterar código para garantir cobertura de teste — encontra unidades testáveis sem teste e CRIA os testes que faltam, seguindo as convenções do repositório. Também invocável sob demanda ("garante os testes", "cria os testes que faltam"). Escreve testes, roda a suíte e reporta o que criou. É o último elo da cadeia de revisão, porque escreve. ([mmacanmunhoz/github-sdd-wrapper](https://github.com/mmacanmunhoz/github-sdd-wrapper/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/implementer-tests.md))
- **implementer-tokens**: pragmatist tokens implementer — composed from the pragmatist personality x tokens domain x implementer phase via /guild-compile. ([krambuhl/agents](https://github.com/krambuhl/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/guild/agents/implementer-tokens.md))
- **implementer-top**: Ralph Plan build-loop worker on the session's strongest model, for large or high-risk steps and for steps a routine implementer failed. Implements one plan step, runs its Verify command, never commits. ([dawsman/ralph-plan](https://github.com/dawsman/ralph-plan/blob/HEAD/plugins/ralph-plan/agents/implementer-top.md))
- **implementer-typescript**: Expert typescript developer implementing features following best practices ([thisisqubika/vitness-AI-CHALLENGE-WC](https://github.com/thisisqubika/vitness-AI-CHALLENGE-WC/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-typescript.md))
- **implementer-ui**: Use to build or change UI: components, pages, layout, styling, accessibility, and i18n wiring (moving strings into message files, hooking up translations). One ticket per agent, in its own worktree. Not for engine logic, API or schema work. ([EliRobinson/ai-utils](https://github.com/EliRobinson/ai-utils/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-roster/agents/implementer-ui.md))
- **implementer-ui**: Phase 2 UI層実装担当。app/内のページ・コンポーネントをタスク内容に沿って実装する。他レイヤー(db/data)のファイルは変更しない。 ([huuriekaiseki-sketch/riff-gear](https://github.com/huuriekaiseki-sketch/riff-gear/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-ui.md))
- **implementer-ui**: Executes ONE UI task unit from an Implementation Plan — client/** (Next.js 15 App Router + React 19 + TanStack Query + next-intl, CSS design tokens). Designed to run MANY-in-parallel: each instance works in its own git worktree, touches only the files its task unit names, applies its preloaded UI skill set, makes the relevant tests pass, and self-reviews ONLY the code it wrote. Use for a planned, file-scoped task tagged `track: ui`; NOT for backend work (use implementer-backend) and NOT for open-ended planning. ([RostK/dev-digest](https://github.com/RostK/dev-digest/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-ui.md))
- **implementer-untested**: Implements one task without a ready test, on the stronger model. ([paranoica/bedrock](https://github.com/paranoica/bedrock/blob/HEAD/plugins/bedrock/agents/implementer-untested.md) · ★3 · 3 copies)
- **implementer-vision**: Implementer — делает по написанному плану ([a3ka/dev-harness](https://github.com/a3ka/dev-harness/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/implementer-vision.md))
- **implementer-watch-protocol**: This doctrine stays **Lupin-first** until cross-project reach is proven. Promote it (move to `planning-is-prompting/workflow/implementer-watch-protocol.md`) when **Either**: ([deepily/lupin](https://github.com/deepily/lupin/blob/HEAD/src/docs/agents/implementer-watch-protocol.md))
- **implementer-web**: Implements ONE approved feature spec in the web frontend, test-first. Use only after Gate 1 approval. Not for spikes, spec edits, backend work, or multi-feature batches. ([Davidcparrar/harness-sdd](https://github.com/Davidcparrar/harness-sdd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-web.md))
- **implementer-wide**: Writes code for ONE task labelled wide (or closed, when the orchestrator passes a stronger model) from the approved plan. Use only when the plan assigns this helper. ([bn-basel/claude-code-orchestrator](https://github.com/bn-basel/claude-code-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/implementer-wide.md))
- **implementer-worker**: Executes ONE bounded task from the planner's plan in an isolated context (supervisor-worker topology). Has Edit/Write/Bash. Returns a typed diff summary. Invoke once per task. ([Camilool8/harness-engineering-templates](https://github.com/Camilool8/harness-engineering-templates/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness-base/agents/implementer-worker.md))
- **implementer-xhigh**: Исполнитель listik-pipeline, effort xhigh: пишет код по карточке. Тело роли — references/implementer.md. ([dmitry-fomin/listik](https://github.com/dmitry-fomin/listik/blob/HEAD/plugins/listik-pipeline/agents/implementer-xhigh.md) · ★5)
- **implementer-xhigh**: orchestra:implementer at xhigh effort. Use only when an Orchestra skill dispatches a hard pre-planned task with a brief. ([lsy041015/orchestra](https://github.com/lsy041015/orchestra/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer-xhigh.md) · ★4)
- **implementer-xhigh**: Implementation agent at xhigh effort, any repo. Use for known-hard packages (unwinder/CFI semantics, cross-crate invariants, subtle concurrency) or to retry a failed implementer run. ([auric/arbareto](https://github.com/auric/arbareto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer-xhigh.md))
- **implementer-xhigh**: Like implementer, with Opus at xhigh effort. Only to escalate work that already came back from implementer-high. ([Lorenzo-Zuin/claude-plugins](https://github.com/Lorenzo-Zuin/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/orchestra/agents/implementer-xhigh.md))
- **implementer-xhigh**: The implementer at xhigh effort, with the same procedure. Use only for one fix round, when a PR's second needs-fix verdict names a behaviour defect (the code does the wrong thing). Every other task goes to implementer. ([spy4x/dotfiles](https://github.com/spy4x/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/ai-harnesses/agents/implementer-xhigh.md))
- **implementer.agent**: ../../src/copilot-cli/agents/implementer.agent.md ([rjmurillo/ai-agents](https://github.com/rjmurillo/ai-agents/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/implementer.agent.md) · ★49)
- **implementer.agent**: Use this agent when dispatching implementation tasks to a subagent in an isolated worktree — verification scales with the task's risk tier (the verification ladder), not a universal test-first ceremony. &lt;example&gt; Context: Orchestrator is dispatching a task from an implementation plan user: "Implement the agent spec handler (task-003)" assistant: "I'll dispatch the exarchos-implementer agent to implement this task on the verification ladder in an isolated worktree." &lt;commentary&gt; An implementation task at any verification tier triggers the implementer agent. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([lvlup-sw/exarchos](https://github.com/lvlup-sw/exarchos/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/implementer.agent.md) · ★19)
- **implementer.agent**: Default coder for one phase of an ai-plans/ implementation plan in the Vinta Schedule API codebase (Django 6 + DRF + Strawberry GraphQL, multi-tenant via SingleOrganizationModelMixin, Postgres with raw-SQL migration framework, DI via dependency-injector, Celery). Reads AGENTS.md + the phase body, executes Changes / Tests / Acceptance, runs the inner loop + outer gate, reports back. Never branches, pushes, or opens PRs (the orchestrator owns git remotes). Never writes AI co-author trailers. ([vintasoftware/vinta-schedule-api](https://github.com/vintasoftware/vinta-schedule-api/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/implementer.agent.md) · ★9)
